> Heratio Help Center article. Category: Exhibitions.

# Exhibition Management

## User Guide

Plan, organize, and manage exhibitions from concept to closing, including object selection, storyline creation, and event scheduling.

---

---

## Key Features
- WHAT YOU CAN DO
- - Create and manage exhibition records
- - Track exhibition through lifecycle stages
- - Select and place objects from your collection
- - Create sections to organize gallery spaces
- - Build storylines and narrative tours
- - Schedule public and private events
- - Track tasks with checklists
- - Generate object lists for installation
- - Manage venues and gallery spaces

## Creating an Exhibition

### Step 1: Basic Information

Go to **Exhibitions** --> **Create Exhibition**

- NEW EXHIBITION
- Title*:        [__________________________]
- Subtitle:      [__________________________]
- Type:          [Temporary Exhibition      v]
- - Permanent
- - Temporary
- - Traveling
- - Online/Virtual
- - Pop-up
- Description:   [__________________________]
- [__________________________]
- [__________________________]
- Theme:         [__________________________]

### Step 2: Dates and Venue

- SCHEDULE & LOCATION
- Planning Start:    [2026-01-15]
- Opening Date:      [2026-06-01]
- Closing Date:      [2026-09-30]
- Venue:             [Main Gallery            v]
- or
- External Venue:    [__________________________]
- Address:           [__________________________]
- City:              [__________________________]

### Step 3: Team and Budget

- ORGANIZATION
- Curator:           [__________________________]
- Organized by:      [__________________________]
- Project Code:      [__________________________]
- Budget:            [____________] [ZAR v]
- Notes:             [__________________________]
- [__________________________]
- [Cancel]  [Save Exhibition]

### Changing Status

1. Open the exhibition detail page
2. Valid status transitions appear as buttons
3. Click the desired transition
4. Optionally add a reason for the change

```
Current Status: [PLANNING]

Available Transitions:
  [Return to Concept]  [Begin Preparation]  [Cancel Exhibition]
```

---

## Managing Exhibition Objects

### Adding Objects

1. Go to exhibition --> **Objects** tab
2. Click **Add Object**
3. Search for objects by identifier or title

### Object Placement Details

When adding or editing an object in the exhibition:

**Object Placement**

- Section:           [Gallery A - Introduction  v]
- Display Position:  [West Wall, Position 3    ]
- Display Order:     [3]
- -- Loan Information --
- Requires Loan:     [x]
- Lender:            [National Museum          ]
- -- Display Requirements --
- Display Case:      [x] Required
- Special Mount:     [ ] Required
- Special Lighting:  [x] Required
- Max Lux Level:     [50]
- -- Insurance --
- Insurance Value:   [250000] ZAR
- -- Labels --
- Label Text:        [________________________]
- Credits:           [________________________]

### Object Statuses

Track objects through the exhibition process:

| STATUS | DESCRIPTION |
|---|---|
| Proposed | Object suggested for exhibition |
| Confirmed | Selection approved |
| Loan Requested | Loan request sent to lender |
| Installed | Object physically installed |
| Removed | Object removed from display |
| Returned | Loaned object returned to lender |

---

## Exhibition Sections

Organize your exhibition space into logical sections.

### Creating Sections

1. Go to exhibition --> **Sections** tab
2. Click **Add Section**

- NEW SECTION
- Title*:            [Introduction                  ]
- Subtitle:          [Welcome to the Exhibition     ]
- Section Type:      [Gallery                      v]
- - Gallery
- - Room
- - Alcove
- - Corridor
- - Outdoor
- - Virtual
- Gallery Name:      [Gallery A                     ]
- Floor Level:       [Ground Floor                  ]
- Square Meters:     [45                            ]
- Theme:             [Setting the Scene             ]
- Color Scheme:      [Neutral tones                 ]
- Narrative:         [________________________]
- [This section introduces...]

### Section Order

Drag and drop sections to reorder them, or specify display order numbers.

---

## Storylines and Narratives

Create guided tours or thematic paths through your exhibition.

### Storyline Types
| TYPE | DESCRIPTION |
|---|---|
| General | Standard exhibition narrative |
| Guided Tour | For docent-led tours |
| Self-Guided | For visitor self-paced tours |
| Educational | For school groups |
| Accessible | For visitors with accessibility needs |
| Highlights | Quick tour of key objects |
| Thematic | Organized by theme |
| Chronological | Organized by date |
| Biographical | Organized around a person's life |

### Creating a Storyline

1. Go to exhibition --> **Storylines** tab
2. Click **Create Storyline**

- NEW STORYLINE
- Title*:            [Highlights Tour                ]
- Type:              [Highlights                    v]
- Target Audience:   [General                       v]
- - General
- - Children
- - Students
- - Specialists
- - All
- Duration:          [30] minutes
- Primary:           [x] This is the main storyline
- Introduction:      [________________________]

### Adding Stops to a Storyline

Link exhibition objects to create a tour path:

## Events Management

Schedule events associated with your exhibition.

### Event Types
| TYPE | EXAMPLE |
|---|---|
| Opening | Exhibition opening reception |
| Closing | Final day celebration |
| Tour | Curator-led tour |
| Lecture | Expert talk on exhibition theme |
| Workshop | Hands-on activity |
| Performance | Live music or theatrical performance |
| Family | Family-friendly activities |
| School | Educational program for schools |
| VIP | Private viewing for donors/members |
| Press | Media preview |

### Creating an Event

- NEW EVENT
- Title*:            [Curator's Talk: Behind the Scenes]
- Type:              [Lecture                       v]
- Date*:             [2026-06-15]
- Start Time:        [14:00]
- End Time:          [15:30]
- Location:          [Auditorium                     ]
- Max Attendees:     [50]
- Registration:      [x] Required
- Free Event:        [ ]
- Ticket Price:      [50] ZAR
- Presenter:         [Dr. Jane Smith                 ]
- Description:       [________________________]

---

## Checklists

Track tasks throughout the exhibition lifecycle.

### Checklist Types

Checklists are organized by exhibition phase:

| PHASE | TYPICAL TASKS |
|---|---|
| Planning | - Define theme and narrative |
|  | - Identify target audience |
|  | - Set preliminary budget |
|  | - Initial object selection |
| Preparation | - Finalize object list |
|  | - Submit loan requests |
|  | - Develop design brief |
|  | - Write interpretive text |
| Installation | - Install display furniture |
|  | - Set lighting |
|  | - Install objects |
|  | - Complete condition reports |
|  | - Install labels and graphics |
|  | - Test AV equipment |
| Opening | - Press release sent |
|  | - VIP invitations |
|  | - Staff briefing |
|  | - Opening event arranged |
| During | - Monitor visitor numbers |
|  | - Environmental monitoring |
|  | - Regular condition checks |
|  | - Gather visitor feedback |
| Closing | - Final visitor statistics |
|  | - Post-exhibition photography |
|  | - Final condition reports |
|  | - Coordinate loan returns |
| Deinstallation | - Remove objects |
|  | - Return all loans |
|  | - Archive documentation |
|  | - Budget reconciliation |
|  | - Team debrief |

### Using Checklists

**CHECKLIST: Installation (Progress: 75%)**

- [x] Install display furniture              John, Jan 15
- [x] Set lighting                           Mary, Jan 16
- [x] Install objects - Gallery A            John, Jan 17
- [x] Install objects - Gallery B            John, Jan 18
- [ ] Complete condition reports             -- Assigned: Mary
- [x] Install labels and graphics            Sue, Jan 19
- [ ] Test AV equipment                      -- Assigned: Tom
- [ ] Security walkthrough                   -- Not started
- Progress: [=================     ] 75%
- [+ Add Task]

---

## Object List Report

Generate a comprehensive list of all objects for installation planning.

### Generating the Report

1. Go to exhibition --> **Object List** tab
2. Click **Generate Report**
3. Choose format: Screen view, CSV, or Print

## Common Tasks

### Finding an Exhibition

1. Go to **Exhibitions** --> **Browse**
2. Use filters:
   - Status (Open, Planning, etc.)
   - Type (Temporary, Permanent, etc.)
   - Year
   - Search by title/theme

### Extending an Exhibition

1. Open the exhibition
2. Edit the closing date
3. Update status if needed (e.g., from Closing back to Open)

### Canceling an Exhibition

1. Open the exhibition
2. Click **Cancel Exhibition**
3. Provide a reason for cancellation
4. Objects will be released

---

## Tips and Best Practices
| DO | DON'T |
|---|---|
| Plan well in advance | Rush through planning phases |
| Keep object list updated | Forget to track loans |
| Document everything | Skip condition reports |
| Use checklists systematically | Rely on memory |
| Create multiple storylines | Neglect accessibility options |
| Track insurance values | Underestimate budget |
| Archive after closing | Delete exhibition data |

---

## Need Help?

- Check the exhibition workflow diagram
- Review checklist templates for guidance
- Contact your system administrator for technical support
